bn=JX(JO(bn,bS),7)bO=((bO+bX))%Jt bV=JX(JO(bV,bO),16)bj=((bj+bV))%Jt bX=JX(JO(bX,bj),12)bO=((bO+bX))%Jt bV=JX(JO(bV,bO),8)bj=((bj+bV))%Jt bX=JX(JO(bX,bj),7)end return{((bc+by))%Jt,((bt+bQ))%Jt;((be+bo))%Jt;((bO+bh))%Jt,((bX+bI))%Jt;((bD+bz))%Jt,((bp+bf))%Jt;((bn+bH))%Jt;((bS+bJ))%Jt,((bj+bb))%Jt,((bC+br))%Jt,((bM+bG))%Jt;((bA+ba))%Jt;((bl+bP))%Jt,((bV+bx))%Jt,((bF+bK))%Jt}end local function mW(mT,md,mq,mv,mB,mU)if#mT==0 then return mT end mq,mv,mB,mU=mq or 1,mv or 0,mB or 0,mU or 0 local mw,mk={},1 while mk<=#mT do local mL=bL(md,mq,mv,mB,mU)for mN=1,16,1 do local mY=mL[mN]for mR=1,4,1 do if mk>#mT then break end mw[mk]=JK(JO(Jx(mT,mk),mY%256))mY,mk=JP(mY/256),mk+1 end end mq=((mq+1))%Jt end return Jc(mw)end local function mE(mi,my,mQ)return bL(mi,0,my,mQ%Jt,JP(mQ/Jt))end local function mo(mh,mI,mz,mf,mH)local mJ=mE(mH,mz,mf)local mb={JO(mI,mJ[1])}for mr=1,8,1 do mb[mr+1]=JO(mh[mr],mJ[mr+1])end mb[10]=mJ[10]return mb end local function mG(ma,mP,mx,mK)local mc=mE(mK,mP,mx)return{JO(ma,mc[1]),mc[10]}end local function me(mO,mX,mD,mp,mn)local mS=mE(mn,mD,mp)return mO[2]==mS[10]and mO[1]==JO(mX,mS[1])end local function mj(mC,mM,mA,ml,mV)local mF=mE(mV,mA,ml)if mC[10]~=mF[10]or mC[1]~=JO(mM,mF[1])then return nil end local mg={}for rW=1,8,1 do mg[rW]=JO(mC[rW+1],mF[rW+1])end return mg end local function rT(rd)local rq={}for rv=1,#rd,1 do local rB=rd[rv]rq[rv]=JK(rB%256,JP(rB/256)%256,JP(rB/65536)%256,JP(rB/16777216)%256)end return Jc(rq)end return{block=bL;crypt=mW;seal=mo,sealAdmission=mG,admit=me;resolve=mj;packWords=rT}end))()local rw={2353773135;177527654,3717961351,3110557305,3927723080,3720518404;880095856,292565808}local function rk(rL,rN,rY)local rR=(Wd(1729096614))local rE={}local ri=Wq[Wd(1075522228)][Wd(1451594495)]for ry=1,#rR,1 do rE[ri(rR,ry)]=ry-1 end local rQ=Wq[Wd(1075522228)][Wd(1467783608)]local ro=Wq[Wd(1670747718)][Wd(1476825026)]local rh=Wq[Wd(1075522228)][Wd(918129822)]local rI=Wq[Wd(354054812)][Wd(872611407)]local rz=Wq[Wd(354054812)][Wd(866109853)]local function rf(rH)return rU[Wd(125777446)](rH,rN,rY)end if Wq[Wd(34946693)](rL)~=(Wd(1075522228))then return rL end local rJ=rQ(rL)local rb={}local rr=1 while rr<=rJ do local rG=(rJ-rr)+1 local ra=rG>=5 and 5 or rG local rP=0 local rx=ra>1 for rK=0,4,1 do local rc if rK<ra then rc=rE[ri(rL,rr+rK)]if not rc then rx=false break end else rc=84 end rP=rP*85+rc end if rx then local rt=ro(rP/16777216)%256 local re=ro(rP/65536)%256 local rO=ro(rP/256)%256 local rX=rP%256 if ra==5 then rI(rb,rh(rt,re,rO,rX))elseif ra==4 then rI(rb,rh(rt,re,rO))elseif ra==3 then rI(rb,rh(rt,re))elseif ra==2 then rI(rb,rh(rt))end end rr=rr+ra end return rf(rz(rb))end local rD={}local Gy=((function(rp,rn,rS,rj)local rC=rk(rS,rj)local rM,rA=Wq[Wd(1075522228)][Wd(1451594495)],Wq[Wd(1075522228)][Wd(89693658)]local function rl(rV,rF)local rg,GW,GT,Gd=rM(rV,rF,rF+3)return((rg+GW*256)+GT*65536)+Gd*16777216 end local Gq={}local Gv,GB=1,1 for GU=1,#rC,4 do local Gw=rl(rC,GU)local Gk=Gw%65536 local GL=Wq[Wd(1670747718)][Wd(1476825026)](Gw/65536)Gq[Gk]={Gv,GL,GB}Gv,GB=Gv+GL,GB+1 end rC=nil return function(GN)local GY=Gq[GN]if not GY then return nil end local GR=rA(rp,GY[1],(GY[1]+GY[2])-1)GR=rk(GR,rn,GY[3])if#GR~=8 and#GR~=40 then return nil end local GE={}for Gi=1,#GR/4,1 do GE[Gi]=rl(GR,((Gi-1))*4+1)end return GE end
